<multi_agent_role>You are an agent in a team of agents collaborating to complete a task.

You can spawn sub-agents to handle subtasks, and those sub-agents can spawn their own sub-agents. All agents in the team, including the agents that you can assign tasks to, are equally intelligent and capable, and have access to the same set of tools.

You can use `spawn_agent` to create a new agent, `followup_task` to give an existing agent a new task and trigger a turn, and `send_message` to pass a message to a running agent.
`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.
Child agents can also spawn their own sub-agents.

When you provide a response in the final channel, that content is immediately delivered back to your parent agent.
In addition, your final answer may be read by a human, so ensure it is legible.

You will receive messages in the analysis channel in the form:
```
Message Type: NEW_TASK | MESSAGE | FINAL_ANSWER
Task name: <recipient>
Sender: <author>
Payload:
<payload text>
```
You may also see them addressed as to=/root/..., which indicates your identity is /root/...

Note that collaboration tools cannot be called from inside `functions.exec`. Call `spawn_agent`, `send_message`, `followup_task`, `wait_agent`, `interrupt_agent`, and `list_agents` only as direct tool calls using the recipient shown in their tool definitions, such as `to=functions.collaboration.spawn_agent`, since they are intentionally absent from the `functions.exec` `tools.*` namespace. Available tools in `functions.exec` are explicitly described with a `tools` namespace in the developer message.

All agents share the same directory. In detail:
- All agents have access to the same container and filesystem as you.
- All agents use the same current working directory.
- As a result, edits made by one agent are immediately visible to all other agents.

When calling `wait_agent`, prefer longer waits (minutes) to avoid busy polling.

There are 4 available concurrency slots, meaning that up to 4 agents can be active at once, including you.

Full-history forks (`fork_turns` omitted or `"all"`) inherit the parent model and reasoning effort and do not accept overrides. Only set `model` or `reasoning_effort` when explicitly requested by the user, applicable `AGENTS.md` instructions, or skill instructions; when doing so, set `fork_turns` to `"none"` or a positive integer string.</multi_agent_role>

Nothing else did:\\r\\n * a student mid-way through a Smart Drill who closed the browser came back to\\r\\n * a fresh shuffle, and the answers they had already given were nowhere on the\\r\\n * screen. The attempts themselves were never lost — those are written to the\\r\\n * `attempts` table per question — but the run was, which reads as lost work.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * This rides the exact same store as the other two, namespaced so the three can\\r\\n * never collide:\\r\\n *\\r\\n *     practice set   →  pool_slug \\"mc-practice-1\\"\\r\\n *     guided drill   →  pool_slug \\"guided::<assignmentId>\\"\\r\\n *     bootcamp day   →  pool_slug \\"drill::bootcamp-day::<day>::<part>\\"\\r\\n *     homework       →  pool_slug \\"drill::assignment::<assignmentId>\\"\\r\\n *     everything else→  pool_slug \\"drill::<mode>::<fingerprint>\\"\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Failure policy, inherited: reads degrade to null (a missing resume row must\\r\\n * never block starting a drill), writes surface to the caller.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  loadPracticeSession,\\r\\n  savePracticeSessionSnapshot,\\r\\n  completePracticeSession,\\r\\n  type PracticeSessionSnapshot,\\r\\n} from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/lib/practiceSessionStore\';\\r\\nimport type { PracticeSession } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX = \'drill::\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type DrillResumeSession = PracticeSession;\\r\\nexport type DrillResumeSnapshot = PracticeSessionSnapshot;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type DrillResumeIdentity = {\\r\\n  kind: \'bootcamp-day\';\\r\\n  day: number;\\r\\n  part: \'homework\' | \'practice\';\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Short, stable, filename-safe digest of whatever identifies the run. */\\r\\nfunction fingerprint(input: string): string {\\r\\n  let h = 2166136261;\\r\\n  for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) {\\r\\n    h ^= input.charCodeAt(i);\\r\\n    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return (h >>> 0).toString(36);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Key one run\'s resume row.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * A homework assignment is identified by the assignment: two assignments of\\r\\n * the same section are two sittings, and a row named by the section alone let\\r\\n * the second one open on the first one\'s answers and the first one\'s spent\\r\\n * clock. Otherwise a full section is identified by the section itself; a\\r\\n * filtered drill by the filters that drew it; Smart Drill by nothing but its\\r\\n * mode, because it has a single continuous run per student. []) {\\r\\n        try {\\r\\n          await this.local.set(row.table_name, row.record_id, row.data as object);\\r\\n        } catch (e) {\\r\\n          console.warn(\'[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] hydrate row failed\', e);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      this.hydrated = true;\\r\\n    } catch (e) {\\r\\n      console.warn(\'[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] hydrate threw\', e);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  private scheduleFlush(): void {\\r\\n    if (this.flushTimer) clearTimeout(this.flushTimer);\\r\\n    this.flushTimer = setTimeout(() => {\\r\\n      void this.flush();\\r\\n    }, FLUSH_DEBOUNCE_MS);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async flush(): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    if (!this.userId || this.userId === \'anon\') return;\\r\\n    if (this.pending.size === 0) return;\\r\\n    const }));\\r\\n        const { error } = await supabase\\r\\n          .from(\'bootcamp_kv\')\\r\\n          .upsert(rows, { onConflict: \'user_id,table_name,record_id\' });\\r\\n        if (error) {\\r\\n Safe to call from anywhere. */\\r\\nexport function loadRcIndex(): Promise<RcIndex> {\\r\\n  if (!indexPromise) {\\r\\n    indexPromise = fetchJson<RcIndex>(\'/data/rc/rc-index.json\').catch((err) => {\\r\\n      indexPromise = null; // let a later call retry rather than caching a failure\\r\\n      throw err;\\r\\n    });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return indexPromise;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Loads (and caches) the flat question search index used by `/rc/search`. */\\r\\nexport function loadRcQuestionIndex(): Promise<RcQuestionIndex> {\\r\\n  if (!questionIndexPromise) {\\r\\n    questionIndexPromise = fetchJson<RcQuestionIndex>(\'/data/rc/rc-question-index.json\').catch(\\r\\n      (err) => {\\r\\n        questionIndexPromise = null; // let a later call retry rather than caching a failure\\r\\n        throw err;\\r\\n      },\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return questionIndexPromise;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Loads (and caches) one PrepTest\'s passages and questions. */\\r\\nexport function loadPreptest(preptest: number): Promise<RcPreptestFile> {\\r\\n  const cached = preptestCache.get(preptest);\\r\\n  if (cached) return cached;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const promise = fetchJson<RcPreptestFile>(`/data/rc/RC-PT${preptest}.json`).catch((err) => {\\r\\n    preptestCache.delete(preptest);\\r\\n    throw err;\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  preptestCache.set(preptest, promise);\\r\\n  return promise;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Finds which PrepTest a passage belongs to, then loads that passage in full. */\\r\\nexport async function loadPassage(passageId: number): Promise<RcPassage | null> {\\r\\n  const index = await loadRcIndex();\\r\\n  const pt = index.preptests.find((t) => t.passages.some((p) => p.passageId === passageId));\\r\\n  if (!pt) return null;\\r\\n  const file = await loadPreptest(pt.preptest);\\r\\n  return file.passages.find((p) => p.passageId === passageId) ?? null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Loads several passages, one PrepTest fetch per distinct test, order preserved. */\\r\\nexport async function loadPassages(passageIds: number[]): Promise<RcPassage[]> {\\r\\n  const index = await loadRcIndex();\\r\\n  const ptByPassage = new Map<number, number>();\\r\\n  for (const pt of index.preptests) {\\r\\n    for (const p of pt.passages) ptByPassage.set(p.passageId, pt.preptest);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const needed = [...new Set(passageIds.map((id) => ptByPassage.get(id)).filter(Boolean))];\\r\\n  const files = await Promise.all(needed.map((n) => loadPreptest(n as number)));\\r\\n  const byId = new Map<number, RcPassage>();\\r\\n  for (const f of files) for (const p of f.passages) byId.set(p.passageId, p);\\r\\n  return passageIds.map((id) => byId.get(id)).filter(Boolean) as RcPassage[];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Every passage in the index, flattened, with its PrepTest attached. */\\r\\nexport async function loadAllIndexPassages(): Promise<(RcIndexPassage & { preptest: number })[]> {\\r\\n  const index = await loadRcIndex();\\r\\n  return index.preptests.flatMap((pt) => pt.passages.map((p) => ({ ...p, preptest: pt.preptest })));\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n"}}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"i":2,"result":{"chunk_id":"677c0f","wall_time_seconds":0.9812741,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":855,"output":"    }\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  const discard = async (run: PracticeTestRun) => {\\r\\n    if (!user) return;\\r\\n    setActionError(null);\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      await abandonAccountPracticeTestRun(user.id, run);\\r\\n      await runsQuery.refetch();\\r\\n    } catch {\\r\\n      setActionError(\\"This exam could not be discarded. ## assistant

The LR section already has an account-synced resume row, but it does not store the selected clock length. RC has account-backed answers, while its clock resets on reload. I’m fixing those gaps and exposing the section history to the new pages.

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"i":0,"result":{"chunk_id":"939c6c","wall_time_seconds":0.329113,"exit_code":1,"original_token_count":0,"output":""}}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"i":1,"result":{"chunk_id":"7655d4","wall_time_seconds":0.322381,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":948,"output":"\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Close out one leg of a practice test.\\r\\n   *\\r\\n   * Order is load-bearing: attempts, then the journal, then section_history,\\r\\n   * then the resume record. The invigilator reads a section_history row as\\r\\n   * \\"this leg is finished\\" and advances past it, so writing history before the\\r\\n   * journal is complete would make the journal retry unreachable. And the\\r\\n   * resume record is finalized only after history lands, or a failed persist\\r\\n   * would leave a completed resume row that re-entry rejects, stranding a leg\\r\\n   * that can never be scored.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  const runSittingCloseout = async (\\r\\n    attempts: DrillSession[\'attempts\'],\\r\\n    finishedSession: DrillSession,\\r\\n  ) => {\\r\\n    const back = drillReturnTo || \'/classroom\';\\r\\n    const key =\\r\\n      user && ptRun\\r\\n        ? closeoutKey(user.id, ptRun.runId, ptRun.legIndex)\\r\\n        : null;\\r\\n\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      if (!user) throw new Error(\'sitting closeout without a signed-in user\');\\r\\n      if (!ptRun) {\\r\\n        // A sitting whose launch state lost its identity is a bug, not a case\\r\\n        // to paper over with a guessed key.\\r\\n        throw new Error(\'sitting closeout without run identity\');\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      const stats = buildSectionStats(finishedSession, undefined);\\r\\n      const expectedWaj = wajQidsForSection(stats);\\r\\n\\r\\n      const { savedQids, wajQids } = await commitSessionAttempts(\\r\\n        attempts,\\r\\n        \'full-section\',\\r\\n      );\\r\\n      let progress = recordCloseoutProgress(key!, { savedQids, wajQids });\\r\\n\\r\\n      // commitSessionAttempts journals only ANSWERED misses. The unanswered\\r\\n      // ones are misses too, and nothing else records them once the results\\r\\n      // screen stops rendering.\\r\\n      const owed = outstandingWajQids(expectedWaj, progress);\\r\\n      if (owed.length > 0) {\\r\\n        const written = await logSectionWrongAnswers({\\r\\n          stats,\\r\\n          qids: owed,\\r\\n          classId,\\r\\n        });\\r\\n        progress = recordCloseoutProgress(key!, { wajQids: written });\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Incomplete journal means the leg is not finished. Leave the key, write\\r\\n      // no history row, and let re-entry retry only what is still owed.\\r\\n      const stillOwed = outstandingWajQids(expectedWaj, progress);\\r\\n      if (stillOwed.length > 0) {\\r\\n        console.error(\'[drill:sitting-closeout] journal incomplete\', {\\r\\n          runId: ptRun.runId,\\r\\n          legIndex: ptRun.legIndex,\\r\\n          owed: stillOwed.length,\\r\\n        });\\r\\n        return;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      if (!progress.historySaved) {\\r\\n        const sectionHistoryId = await persistSectionResults({\\r\\n          session: finishedSession,\\r\\n          stats,\\r\\n          classId,\\r\\n          userId: user.id,\\r\\n        });\\r\\n        // Every leg of a practice test is a section, and each one gets its own\\r\\n        // report. Not awaited: the leg is saved, and the sitting has to be able\\r\\n        // to move on to the next section whatever the mail server is doing.\\r\\n        void emailSectionReport(sectionHistoryId);\\r\\n        progress = recordCloseoutProgress(key!, { historySaved: true });\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      await finalizeDrillResumeSession(attempts);\\r\\n      clearCloseoutProgress(key!);\\r\\n    } catch (error) {\\r\\n      console.error(\'[drill:sitting-closeout] failed\', error);\\r\\n    } finally {\\r\\n      navigate(back);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  const handleFinishSection = async (\\r\\n    attemptsOverride?: DrillSession[\'attempts\'],\\r\\n    options?: { returnToAfterFinish?: string },\\r\\n  ) => {\\r\\n    if (!session) return;\\r\\n\\r\\n    // The last question never gets left, so its visit would never be banked.\\r\\n    // Close it here or every section under-counts by one.\\r\\n    closeOpenVisit();\\r\\n\\r\\n    // Stop the section timer so it does not keep ticking through\\r\\n    // SectionComplete / blind review / LRSectionResults.\\r\\n    if (timer) timer.pause();\\r\\n"}}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"i":2,"result":{"chunk_id":"831b00","wall_time_seconds":0.3222569,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":121,"output":"  end_of_set: \'at the end\',\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type RcTiming =\\r\\n  | { mode: \'unlimited\' }\\r\\n  | { mode: \'per_passage_budget\'; secondsPerPassage: number };\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface RcDrillConfig {\\r\\n  reveal: RcRevealMode;\\r\\n  timing: RcTiming;\\r\\n  source: \'manual\' | \'adaptive\' | \'assignment\';\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type RcSessionStatus = \'in_progress\' | \'completed\' | \'abandoned\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface RcDrillSession {\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  userId: string;\\r\\n  config: RcDrillConfig;\\r\\n  passageIds: number[];\\r\\n"}}'}]

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu-wt-drill-layout\\src\\lib\\practiceSectionAttempts.ts\n+import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';\n+import { PracticeSession, TABLES } from '@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records';\n+import { loadRcIndex } from '@/features/rc/lib/rcBank';\n+import { getSessionAttempts, listSessions } from '@/features/rc/api/rcProgress';\n+\n+export interface PracticeSectionAttempt {\n+  id: string;\n+  kind: 'lr-section' | 'rc-section';\n+  status: 'in-progress' | 'completed';\n+  preptest: number;\n+  section: number;\n+  startedAt: string;\n+  updatedAt: string;\n+  answered: number;\n+  total: number;\n+  href: string;\n+}\n+\n+/** List only this signed-in student's standalone official sections. */\n+export async function listPracticeSectionAttempts(userId: string): Promise<PracticeSectionAttempt[]> {\n+  const { data: auth, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();\n+  if (authError || !auth.user || auth.user.id !== userId) {\n+    throw new Error('Sign in to see your practice sections.');\n+  }\n+\n+  const { data: lrRows, error: lrError } = await supabase\n+    .from('bootcamp_kv')\n+    .select('data')\n+    .eq('user_id', userId)\n+    .eq('table_name', TABLES.practice_sessions);\n+  if (lrError) throw lrError;\n+\n+  const lr: PracticeSectionAttempt[] = [];\n+  for (const row of lrRows ?? []) {\n+    const parsed = PracticeSession.safeParse(row.data);\n+    if (!parsed.success) continue;\n+    const session = parsed.data;\n+    const match = /^drill::section::pt(\\d+)-s(\\d+)$/.exec(session.pool_slug);\n+    if (!match) continue;\n+    const preptest = Number(match[1]);\n+    const section = Number(match[2]);\n+    const timer = session.timer_mode && session.timer_mode !== '35'\n+      ? `&timer=${encodeURIComponent(session.timer_mode)}`\n+      : '';\n+    const minutes = session.timer_mode === 'custom' && session.timer_custom_minutes\n+      ? `&minutes=${encodeURIComponent(String(session.timer_custom_minutes))}`\n+      : '';\n+    lr.push({\n+      id: session.id,\n+      kind: 'lr-section',\n+      status: session.status === 'completed' ? 'completed' : 'in-progress',\n+      preptest,\n+      section,\n+      startedAt: session.started_at,\n+      updatedAt: session.updated_at,\n+      answered: session.attempts.length,\n+      total: session.question_queue.length,\n+      href: session.status === 'completed'\n+        ? '/history?tab=sections'\n+        : `/drill?mode=full-section&pt=${preptest}&section=${section}${timer}${minutes}&returnTo=%2Ftesting`,\n+    });\n+  }\n+\n+  const [rcSessions, index] = await Promise.all([listSessions(), loadRcIndex()]);\n+  const passageMap = new Map<number, { preptest: number; section: number; questionCount: number }>();\n+  const sectionSize = new Map<string, number>();\n+  for (const pt of index.preptests) {\n+    for (const passage of pt.passages) {\n+      passageMap.set(passage.passageId, {\n+        preptest: pt.preptest,\n+        section: passage.section,\n+        questionCount: passage.questionCount,\n+      });\n+      const key = `${pt.preptest}:${passage.section}`;\n+      sectionSize.set(key, (sectionSize.get(key) ?? 0) + 1);\n+    }\n+  }\n+\n+  const rc = await Promise.all(rcSessions.filter((session) => session.status !== 'abandoned').map(async (session) => {\n+    const passages = session.passageIds.map((id) => passageMap.get(id));\n+    const first = passages[0];\n+    if (!first || passages.some((p) => !p || p.preptest !== first.preptest || p.section !== first.section)) return null;\n+    if (passages.length !== sectionSize.get(`${first.preptest}:${first.section}`)) return null;\n+    const attempts = await getSessionAttempts(session.id);\n+    return {\n+      id: session.id,\n+      kind: 'rc-section' as const,\n+      status: session.status === 'completed' ? 'completed' as const : 'in-progress' as const,\n+      preptest: first.preptest,\n+      section: first.section,\n+      startedAt: session.startedAt,\n+      updatedAt: session.completedAt ?? session.config.lastActiveAt ?? session.startedAt,\n+      answered: new Set(attempts.filter((attempt) => attempt.isFirstAttempt).map((attempt) => attempt.questionId)).size,\n+      total: passages.reduce((sum, passage) => sum + (passage?.questionCount ?? 0), 0),\n+      href: session.status === 'completed'\n+        ? `/rc/results/${session.id}`\n+        : `/rc/drill?sessionId=${session.id}&returnTo=%2Ftesting`,\n+    };\n+  }));\n+\n+  return [...lr, ...rc.filter((item): item is NonNullable<typeof item> => item !== null)]\n+    .sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt.localeCompare(a.updatedAt));\n+}\n*** End Patch"))

Content is static JSON; only\r\n                      progress touches the database. See src/features/rc/CONTEXT.md. */}\r\n                  {/* The RC hub is a section of the drill hub now, chosen by\r\n                      the LR/RC switch. This route survives as a\r\n                      query-preserving adapter — see RcRedirect. */}\r\n                  <Route path="/rc" element={\r\n                    <DrillAssignmentGate><RcRedirect /></DrillAssignmentGate>\r\n                  } />\r\n                  {/* The directions screen before an assigned section. Outside\r\n                      the Classroom shell on purpose: it takes the whole screen,\r\n                      like the section it leads into. */}\r\n                  <Route path="/section/:assignmentId" element={\r\n                    <ProtectedRoute flag="has_classroom_access"><SectionDirectionsPage /></ProtectedRoute>\r\n                  } />\r\n                  <Route path="/rc/drill" element={\r\n                    <DrillAssignmentGate><RcPlayer /></DrillAssignmentGate>\r\n                  } />\r\n                  <Route path="/rc/results/:sessionId" element={\r\n                    <ProtectedRoute flag="has_drill_access"><RcResults /></ProtectedRoute>\r\n                  } />\r\n                  <Route path="/rc/search" element={\r\n                    <ProtectedRoute flag="has_drill_access"><RcSearch /></ProtectedRoute>\r\n                  } />\r\n                  {/* Legacy path — the Wrong Answer Journal is now the Wrong Answers section of the Journal. */}\r\n                  <Route path="/waj" element={<Navigate to="/journal" replace />} />\r\n                  {/* Dev-only harness for the Activity Centre worlds. The runner\r\n                      is behind auth + a feature flag, which makes a world\r\n'}]

The panel stays\r\n  // open across questions once opened, so a teaching pass is one click, not ten.\r\n  const { is_admin: isAdmin } = useUserPermissions();\r\n  const [teachingNotesOpen, setTeachingNotesOpen] = React.useState(false);\r\n  const { settings } = useUserSettings();\r\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\r\n  const { isLoading: qbLoading } = useQuestionBank();\r\n\r\n  // Everything about the run itself comes from the Drill Run Spec, which the\r\n  // gate in front of this page built from the launch in the address bar. The\r\n  // runner does not parse the query string for it: one grammar, one reader.\r\n  const state = useDrillRunSpec();\r\n  const assignmentId = state.assignmentId ?? null;\r\n  const guidedAssignmentId = state.guidedAssignmentId ?? null;\r\n  const guidedDrillId = state.guidedDrillId ?? null;\r\n  const guidedSetId = state.guidedSetId;\r\n  const { set: guidedSet } = useHomeworkSet(guidedSetId);\r\n  const [guidedInfoOpen, setGuidedInfoOpen] = React.useState(false);\r\n\r\n  const practicingLabel = state.practicingLabel;\r\n\r\n  // Context params for header/breadcrumbs (survive refresh)\r\n  const origin = searchParams.get(\'origin\');\r\n  const returnTo = searchParams.get(\'returnTo\');\r\n  const drillReturnTo = state.returnTo ?? (returnTo && returnTo.startsWith(\'/\') && !returnTo.startsWith(\'//\') ? returnTo : undefined);\r\n  // Which run and leg this section belongs to, when it belongs to one. Comes\r\n  // from the launch rather than from returnTo: that string is a routing hint\r\n  // and nothing load-bearing may depend on it. A refreshed leg used to lose\r\n  // these and then had nothing to write its close-out against, so the section\r\n  // could never be marked finished and the practice test stayed open forever.\r\n  const ptRun = state.ptRun;\r\n  // Routing and score-UI suppression only. Never a clock or integrity decision.\r\n  const inSitting = isSittingReturn(drillReturnTo);\r\n  /**\r\n   * Answer and Explanation are not rendered at all for this run.\r\n   *\r\n   * Two runs are sealed. A practice-test sitting, because a sitting reveals\r\n   * nothing until it is scored and the old per-question gate quietly broke\r\n   * that: attempt a question inside a sitting and the reveal unlocked. PtLegIdentity {\r\n  runId: string;\r\n  legIndex: number;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface LrLegLaunch {\r\n  pathname: \'/drill\';\r\n  state: {\r\n    mode: \'full-section\';\r\n    config: PracticeTestLeg,\r\n  legIndex: number,\r\n): LrLegLaunch {\r\n  return {\r\n    pathname: \'/drill\',\r\n    state: {\r\n      mode: \'full-section\',\r\n      config: {\r\n        pt: run.pt,\r\n        section: leg.section,\r\n        timer: {\r\n          ...timerForRun(run),\r\n          isPaused: false,\r\n          elapsedMs: 0,\r\n          startedAt: Date.now(),\r\n        },\r\n      },\r\n      returnTo: practiceTestPath(run.id),\r\n      practicingLabel: `Practice Test ${run.pt}`,\r\n      ptRun: { runId: run.id, legIndex },\r\n    },\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The RC leg\'s link. `reveal=end_of_set` because a section is sat under test\r\n * conditions — nothing is revealed until the sitting is over, and inside a\r\n * practice test that is doubly true.\r\n */\r\nexport function rcLegHref(\r\n  run: PracticeTestRun,\r\n  leg: PracticeTestLeg,\r\n  legIndex: number,\r\n): string {\r\n  const secondsPerPassage = (() => {\r\n    // Same reading as the LR leg: the run\'s own resolved clock, so an\r\n    // accommodated student\'s RC passages get their share of the longer section\r\n    // rather than a share of 35 minutes.\r\n    const sectionSeconds = [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.9 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "import { Navigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';\r\nimport { DRILL_SECTION_PARAM } from '@/lib/drillSection';\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * /rc is now the drill hub's RC section.\r\n *\r\n * The route stays because links to it are in the wild — assignment links\r\n * (`/rc?passages=…`), the old quick-build link (`/rc?count=…&start=1`), and the\r\n * returns hard-coded in RcPlayer and RcResults. Every one of them keeps\r\n * working: the query is carried across verbatim.\r\n *\r\n * A link that carries any builder query also asks for the builder stage. Landing\r\n * a `start=1` link on the hub's default RC stage would drop it on the floor —\r\n * the builder that reads those params would not be mounted to read them.\r\n */\r\nexport default function RcRedirect() {\r\n  const { search, hash } = useLocation();\r\n  const params = new URLSearchParams(search);\r\n  params.delete(DRILL_SECTION_PARAM);\r\n\r\n  const carries = params.toString().length > 0;\r\n  const next = new URLSearchParams(params);\r\n  next.set(DRILL_SECTION_PARAM, 'rc');\r\n  if (carries) next.set('stage', 'type-drill');\r\n\r\n  return <Navigate to={`/classroom/drill?${next.toString()}${hash}`} replace />;\r\n}\r\n"}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '/**\r\n * Handing one leg of a practice test to the runner that already exists, and\r\n * reading its result back.\r\n *\r\n * The sitting owns sequence, clock and scoring. It does NOT own answering —\r\n * that stays in `/drill` (LR) and `/rc` (RC), so a question inside a practice\r\n * test behaves identically to the same question outside one. The cost of that\r\n * choice is this file: the runners report to their own tables, and the sitting\r\n * has to go and find the row afterwards.\r\n *\r\n * A leg is matched by "this user, this section, finished after the moment the\r\n * leg began". That is why `startedAt` is stamped before navigating and never\r\n * after.\r\n */\r\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\r\nimport type { FullSectionConfig, TimerMode } from \'@/types/drill\';\r\nimport {\r\n  practiceTestRunSectionSeconds,\r\n  type PracticeTestLeg,\r\n  type PracticeTestRun,\r\n} from \'@/lib/practiceTest\';\r\nimport { RC_STANDARD_SECONDS_PER_PASSAGE } from \'@/features/rc/lib/rcTypes\';\r\n\r\nconst PRACTICE_TEST_PATH_PREFIX = \'/testing/test/\';\r\n\r\nexport function practiceTestPath(runId: string): string {\r\n  return `${PRACTICE_TEST_PATH_PREFIX}${runId}`;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Whether a return path points back into a sitting.\r\n *\r\n * ROUTING ONLY: it decides where a runner navigates and whether score UI is\r\n * suppressed. It must never gate a clock or an integrity rule, because the path\r\n * it reads is student-editable. Anything load-bearing reads persisted run and\r\n * leg attribution instead.\r\n *\r\n * The prefix lives here, next to the function that writes it, so the two cannot\r\n * drift apart.\r\n */\r\nexport function isSittingReturn(path: string | null | undefined): boolean {\r\n  return !!path?.startsWith(PRACTICE_TEST_PATH_PREFIX);\r\n'}]

A link carrying `start=1` puts\r\n   * the builder on the history stack with that param still in the URL, so Back\r\n   * from the drill re-mounts the builder, re-reads the param, and creates a\r\n   * SECOND session. Replacing the entry takes that URL off the stack.\r\n   */\r\n  const start = async (options?: { replace?: boolean }) => {\r\n    setStarting(true);\r\n    try {\r\n      const chosen =\r\n        handPicked.length > 0\r\n          ? handPicked\r\n          : selectPassages({\r\n              pool: passages,\r\n              questionIdsByPassage,\r\n              filters,\r\n              history,\r\n              count,\r\n              seed: Date.now() % 2147483647,\r\n            }).selected.map((p) => p.passageId);\r\n\r\n      if (chosen.length === 0) {\r\n        setNotice('No passages match those filters. Loosen one and try again.');\r\n        setStarting(false);\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n      const session = await createSession(config, chosen);\r\n      // A link that said where to go afterwards keeps saying it: the practice\r\n      // test hands off its RC leg this way and needs the student back.\r\n      const back = safeReturnTo(params.get('returnTo'));\r\n      navigate(\r\n        '/rc/drill?sessionId=' + session.id + (back ? '&returnTo=' + encodeURIComponent(back) : ''),\r\n        { replace: options?.replace },\r\n      );\r\n    } catch (err) {\r\n      setNotice(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'The drill could not be started.');\r\n      setStarting(false);\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  // Runs a tick after the link has been applied, so it starts on the count and\r\n  // timing the link asked for rather than on the builder defaults.\r\n"}]

Unlimited has\r\n * no budget, which is not the same as a budget of zero.\r\n */\r\nfunction sectionBudgetMs(timer?: TimerConfig): number | undefined {\r\n  if (!timer) return undefined;\r\n  switch (timer.mode) {\r\n    case \'35\':\r\n      return 35 * 60 * 1000;\r\n    case \'52.5\':\r\n      return 52.5 * 60 * 1000;\r\n    case \'70\':\r\n      return 70 * 60 * 1000;\r\n    case \'custom\':\r\n      return timer.customMinutes ? timer.customMinutes * 60 * 1000 : undefined;\r\n    default:\r\n      return undefined;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 2.8 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "  };\r\n\r\n  // UI-level helper for the per-question submit handlers. Wraps the throwing\r\n  // service so the callers' `if (!saved.ok)` toast logic keeps working — the\r\n  // error is logged at the service AND visible to the user via the caller's\r\n  // toast. This does NOT silently swallow: failures are logged, classified\r\n  // where the error still exists, and surfaced through the typed result.\r\n  type PersistResult =\r\n    | { ok: true }\r\n    | { ok: false; errorKind: 'auth' | 'network' | 'db' | 'unknown' };\r\n  const classifyPersistError = (e: unknown): 'auth' | 'network' | 'db' | 'unknown' => {\r\n    const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e ?? '');\r\n    if (/no class_id and no user\\.id|jwt|auth|401|403/i.test(message)) return 'auth';\r\n    if (/fetch|network|timeout|Failed to fetch/i.test(message)) return 'network';\r\n    if (/insert failed|supabase|postgres|duplicate|violates/i.test(message)) return 'db';\r\n    return 'unknown';\r\n  };\r\n  // Structured failure event. No PII: no user id, no answer text. There is no\r\n  // error-reporting sink in this repo as of 2026-09-12, so `console.error` is\r\n  // the sink; swap it here when one lands.\r\n  const reportCommitFailure = (event: {\r\n    qid: string;\r\n    launchKind: string | undefined;\r\n    transition: string;\r\n    errorKind: 'auth' | 'network' | 'db' | 'unknown';\r\n    deduped: boolean;\r\n  }) => {\r\n    console.error('[drill:commit-failed]', event);\r\n  };\r\n  const persistAttempt = async (\r\n    attemptData: Parameters<typeof saveAttemptToDatabase>[0],\r\n  ): Promise<PersistResult> => {\r\n    try {\r\n      const saved = await saveAttemptToDatabase(attemptData);\r\n      return saved ? { ok: true } : { ok: false, errorKind: 'unknown' };\r\n    } catch (e) {\r\n      const errorKind = classifyPersistError(e);\r\n      console.error('[drill:persistAttempt] threw, surfacing as save-failed', {\r\n        qid: attemptData.qid,\r\n        mode: attemptData.mode,\r\n        error: e,\r\n      });\r\n      reportCommitFailure({\r\n        qid: attemptData.qid,\r\n        launchKind: state.launchKind,\r\n        transition: 'persistAttempt',\r\n        errorKind,\r\n        deduped: false,\r\n      });\r\n      return { ok: false, errorKind };\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  // Persists every selected-answer attempt in the session. Per design, this\r\n  // is the gate before a section closes — every attempt MUST hit the DB or\r\n  // we rethrow so handleFinishSection can surface the failure rather than\r\n  // silently completing a section with missing data.\r\n  //\r\n  // timeMs values are checked: a value of 0 (or negative) indicates a broken\r\n  // timer somewhere upstream and is logged + counted, never silently saved.\r\n  const commitSessionAttempts = async (\r\n    attempts: DrillSession['attempts'],\r\n    mode: DrillMode\r\n  ): Promise<{\r\n    saved: number;\r\n    wajFailed: number;\r\n    timeMsZero: number;\r\n    /** Attempt rows that landed, so a retry can skip them. */\r\n    savedQids: string[];\r\n    /** Journal rows that landed. A short list means closeout work is owed. */\r\n    wajQids: string[];\r\n  }> => {\r\n    let saved = 0;\r\n    let wajFailed = 0;\r\n    let timeMsZero = 0;\r\n    const attemptErrors: unknown[] = [];\r\n    const savedQids: string[] = [];\r\n    const wajQids: string[] = [];\r\n\r\n    for (const [qid, attempt] of attempts) {\r\n      const question = questionBank.getQuestion(qid);\r\n      if (!question || !attempt.selectedAnswer) continue;\r\n\r\n      if (!Number.isFinite(attempt.timeMs) || attempt.timeMs <= 0) {\r\n        timeMsZero++;\r\n        console.warn('[commitSessionAttempts] non-positive timeMs', {\r\n          qid,\r\n          timeMs: attempt.timeMs,\r\n        });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      try {\r\n        const ok = await saveAttemptToDatabase({\r\n          qid,\r\n          correct: attempt.correct,\r\n          time_ms: attempt.timeMs,\r\n          qtype: question.qtype,\r\n          level: question.difficulty,\r\n          confidence: attempt.confidence ?? null,\r\n          mode,\r\n          selected_answer: attempt.selectedAnswer,\r\n        });\r\n        if (ok === false) {\r\n          // Cold-start skip: classId not yet resolved. Treat as failure so the\r\n          // section is not closed with attempts silently dropped.\r\n"}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 2.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "  const overBudget = budget != null && clockSeconds < 0;\r\n\r\n  /* --------------------------------------------------------- writing */\r\n\r\n  const writeAttempt = React.useCallback(\r\n    async (target: RcSessionItem, selected: RcLetter | null): Promise<boolean> => {\r\n      if (!sessionId) return false;\r\n      // A question already written once in this session is not written again.\r\n      // Resume used to lose the reveal state, which left Confirm enabled on a\r\n      // question that was already answered and inserted a second attempt row\r\n      // for it. The reveal state is restored now; this is the belt.\r\n      if (state.recorded.has(target.question.questionId)) return true;\r\n      const timeMs = Math.max(0, Date.now() - questionStart.current);\r\n      try {\r\n        await recordAttempt({\r\n          sessionId,\r\n          questionId: target.question.questionId,\r\n          passageId: target.passage.passageId,\r\n          selected,\r\n          isCorrect: selected ? selected === target.question.correctAnswer : null,\r\n          timeMs,\r\n          flagged: state.flagged.has(target.question.questionId),\r\n          eliminations: state.eliminations.get(target.question.questionId) ?? [],\r\n          isFirstAttempt: true,\r\n        });\r\n        dispatch({ type: 'markRecorded', questionIds: [target.question.questionId] });\r\n        return true;\r\n      } catch {\r\n        /*\r\n         * A failed write used to return quietly and the caller carried on:\r\n         * Confirm revealed, the passage moved into review, and submit walked to\r\n         * the results screen — all of it telling the student work was recorded\r\n         * when none of it was. Losing their place is still the wrong cure, so\r\n         * the drill stays where it is and says what happened instead.\r\n         */\r\n        setSaveError('That answer did not save.');\r\n        return false;\r\n      }\r\n    },\r\n    [sessionId, state.flagged, state.eliminations, state.recorded],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  const recordPassage = React.useCallback(\r\n    async (passageId: number) => {\r\n      const items = itemsForPassage(state, passageId).filter(\r\n        (i) => !state.recorded.has(i.question.questionId),\r\n      );\r\n      let allSaved = true;\r\n      for (const target of items) {\r\n        const saved = await writeAttempt(\r\n          target,\r\n          state.answers.get(target.question.questionId) ?? null,\r\n        );\r\n        if (!saved) allSaved = false;\r\n      }\r\n      // Feedback is the reward for a recorded answer. Opening it over a write\r\n      // that failed would show the student a verdict on work nothing holds.\r\n      if (allSaved) dispatch({ type: 'reviewPassage', passageId });\r\n      return allSaved;\r\n    },\r\n    [state, writeAttempt],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  /* ------------------------------------------------------ navigation */\r\n\r\n  const goTo = React.useCallback(\r\n    (index: number) => {\r\n      const leaving = getCurrentItem(state);\r\n      // end_of_set writes silently as the student moves on.\r\n      if (\r\n        leaving &&\r\n        config.reveal === 'end_of_set' &&\r\n        !state.recorded.has(leaving.question.questionId) &&\r\n        state.answers.has(leaving.question.questionId)\r\n      ) {\r\n        void writeAttempt(leaving, state.answers.get(leaving.question.questionId) ?? null);\r\n      }\r\n      dispatch({ type: 'goto', index });\r\n    },\r\n    [state, config.reveal, writeAttempt],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  const handleSubmit = React.useCallback(async () => {\r\n    for (const target of state.items) {\r\n      if (!state.recorded.has(target.question.questionId)) {\r\n        const saved = await writeAttempt(\r\n          target,\r\n          state.answers.get(target.question.questionId) ?? null,\r\n        );\r\n        // Stop at the first failure. Completing the session and walking to a\r\n        // results screen built on answers that were never stored is the worst\r\n        // version of this: the student sees a score for work that is gone.\r\n        if (!saved) return;\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n    dispatch({ type: 'submit' });\r\n    if (sessionId) {\r\n      try {\r\n        await completeSession(sessionId);\r\n      } catch {\r\n        setSaveError('The drill did not finish saving.');\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n      // A leg of a practice test goes straight back to the invigilator. The\r\n      // session is already completed in the database by this point, so the\r\n      // sitting can score it; showing the results screen first would reveal a\r\n      // section score with sections still to sit. RC is frequently section 1\r\n      // or 2, so this is the common case, not an edge one.\r\n      if (isSittingReturn(returnTo)) {\r\n        navigate(returnTo);\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n      // Carried, not dropped: anything else with a returnTo has somewhere to be\r\n      // after the results screen, and only this query string knows where.\r\n      navigate(`/rc/results/${sessionId}${returnTo ? `?returnTo=${encodeURIComponent(returnTo)}` : ''}`);\r\n    }\r\n  }, [state, writeAttempt, sessionId, navigate, returnTo]);\r\n\r\n  const toggleFlag = React.useCallback(\r\n    (questionId: number) => {\r\n      dispatch({ type: 'toggleFlag', questionId });\r\n      if (sessionId) {\r\n        void setAttemptFlag(sessionId, questionId, !state.flagged.has(questionId));\r\n      }\r\n    },\r\n    [sessionId, state.flagged],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  /* ------------------------------------------------- selection, guarded */\r\n\r\n  /**\r\n   * Is this question still waiting on a blind re-attempt?\r\n   *\r\n   * Review of work the student did not just answer starts with a fresh attempt,\r\n   * and until they make it, nothing on screen may state the verdict — not the\r\n"}]

if (e.key === \'ArrowLeft\') {\r\n        e.preventDefault();\r\n        goTo(state.currentIndex - 1);\r\n      } else if (e.key === \'ArrowRight\') {\r\n        e.preventDefault();\r\n        goTo(state.currentIndex + 1);\r\n      } else if (e.key.toLowerCase() === \'f\') {\r\n        e.preventDefault();\r\n        toggleFlag(item.question.questionId);\r\n      } else if (/^[a-eA-E]$/.test(e.key)) {\r\n        e.preventDefault();\r\n        selectLetter(e.key.toUpperCase() as RcLetter);\r\n      }\r\n    };\r\n    window.addEventListener(\'keydown\', onKey);\r\n    return () => window.removeEventListener(\'keydown\', onKey);\r\n  }, [item, modal, helpOpen, state.paused, state.currentIndex, toggleFlag, goTo, selectLetter]);\r\n\r\n  /* ----------------------------------------------------------- render */\r\n\r\n  if (error) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <OfficialDrillFrame notepadEnabled={false}>\r\n        <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-4 p-10 text-center">\r\n          <p className="text-[15px] text-slate-700">{error}</p>\r\n          <button\r\n            type="button"\r\n            className="rounded-full bg-slate-900 px-7 py-2 text-[14px] font-semibold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-black"\r\n            onClick={() => navigate(exitPath)}\r\n          >\r\n            Go to Reading Comprehension\r\n          </button>\r\n        </div>\r\n      </OfficialDrillFrame>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (!passages || !item) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <OfficialDrillFrame notepadEnabled={false}>\r\n        <div className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center p-10">\r\n          <p className="text-[15px] text-slate-600">Loading the passage…</p>\r\n        </div>\r\n      </OfficialDrillFrame>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // `revealed`, `reattemptMode` and `awaitingReattempt` are declared above the\r\n  // early returns, with the guarded selector that shares them.\r\n  const questionId = item.question.questionId;\r\n  const selected = state.answers.get(questionId) ?? null;\r\n\r\n  const passageDone = passageComplete(state, item.passage.passageId);\r\n  const passageReviewed = state.reviewedPassages.has(item.passage.passageId);\r\n\r\n  /**\r\n   * A pager entry\'s verdict as a value. Derived once so the colour classes and\r\n   * the glyph/aria status below can never disagree about what a pill means.\r\n   */\r\n  const pillStatus = (entry: RcPagerEntry): \'correct\' | \'incorrect\' | \'answered\' | \'unanswered\' => {\r\n    if (entry.kind !== \'question\' || entry.questionId == null) return \'unanswered\';\r\n    // A pill that reads "correct" beside a question the student is being asked\r\n    // to answer again blind has already answered it for them.\r\n    if (awaitingReattempt(entry.questionId)) return \'unanswered\';\r\n    const target = state.items.find((i) => i.question.questionId === entry.questionId);\r\n    // The pill states the same verdict the review layer does, which reads the\r\n    // latest pick (`secondPick ?? firstPick`). Reading `answers` alone put a\r\n    // green check beside a question whose banner said "Not quite" — the pill\r\n    // was scoring the first attempt while the banner scored the re-attempt.\r\n    const pick = reattemptPicks.get(entry.questionId) ?? state.answers.get(entry.questionId);\r\n    const showResult = target ? isRevealed(state, config.reveal, target) : false;\r\n    if (showResult && pick && target) {\r\n      return pick === target.question.correctAnswer ? \'correct\' : \'incorrect\';\r\n    }\r\n    return pick ? \'answered\' : \'unanswered\';\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  /** Pill colors for a pager entry that is not the current question. */\r\n  const pillState = (entry: RcPagerEntry): string => {\r\n    if (entry.kind !== \'question\' || entry.questionId == null) return \'\';\r\n    switch (pillStatus(entry)) {\r\n      case \'correct\':\r\n        return \'border-green-600 bg-green-50 text-green-700\';\r\n'}]

This used to live inside `LRSectionResults`, in a mount effect. That was fine\\r\\n * while the only way to finish a section was to look at the results screen, and\\r\\n * wrong the moment a practice test needed to finish a section WITHOUT showing\\r\\n * one: the sitting\'s scorer (`readLrLegResult`) reads back the `section_history`\\r\\n * row that effect wrote, so skipping the screen skipped the row and every leg\\r\\n * read as unfinished.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * So the writing lives here and the screen calls it, rather than the other way\\r\\n * round.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Two behaviours are deliberate and differ from the old inline code:\\r\\n *\\r\\n * 1. `user_id` is set explicitly. The column is nullable with no default and no\\r\\n *    trigger, the old insert omitted it, and `readLrLegResult` filters on it —\\r\\n *    so LR leg readback almost certainly never matched in production.\\r\\n * 2. `persistSectionResults` THROWS on failure. The results screen catches and\\r\\n *    toasts (a standalone student can retry by other means), but the practice\\r\\n *    test closeout must be able to tell that the write failed, because it\\r\\n *    decides whether the leg counts as finished.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport type { Json } from \'@/integrations/supabase/types\';\\r\\nimport { questionBank } from \'@/lib/questionLoader\';\\r\\nimport type { DrillSession, BlindReviewResult } from \'@/types/drill\';\\r\\nimport { analyseSection, type AnalysisInput } from \'@/lib/sectionAnalysis\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/** One question\'s outcome in a sat section. */\\r\\nexport interface SectionQuestionStat {\\r\\n  qid: string;\\r\\n  qnum: number;\\r\\n  qtype: string;\\r\\n  difficulty: number;\\r\\n  initialAnswer: string;\\r\\n  correctAnswer: string;\\r\\n  initialCorrect: boolean;\\r\\n  brAnswer?: string;\\r\\n  brCorrect?: boolean;\\r\\n  brChanged?: boolean;\\r\\n  timeMs: number;\\r\\n  brTimeMs?: number;\\r\\n  switchCount: number;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * What the bank knows about the question, copied onto the sitting so the\\r\\n   * record survives the bank changing under it. A report read next year has to\\r\\n   * describe the question as it was when it was sat, and re-reading the bank at\\r\\n   * display time cannot promise that.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  reasoningTypes?: string[];\\r\\n  questionFeatures?: string[];\\r\\n  /** How many times the student opened it, and how many of those changed nothing. */\\r\\n  visitCount?: number;\\r\\n  noChangeRevisits?: number;\\r\\n  /** Choices crossed off. Three of them is \\"down to two\\", the flag criterion. */\\r\\n  eliminations?: string[];\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Did they ever open it? A question worked and left blank is a decision; one\\r\\n   * never reached is the clock. Both are \\"unanswered\\" without this.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  everVisited?: boolean;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Every choice put down on this question, in order, each stamped with how\\r\\n   * far into the question it was made. The results screen draws it as a\\r\\n   * timeline; an unanswered question has none.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  answerTrail?: Array<{ answer: string; atMs: number }>;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Answered after the section clock ran out. Undefined for a section that\\r\\n   * never ran out, which is not the same as false on one that did.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  answeredInOvertime?: boolean;\\r\\n  flagged: boolean;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** The answer a student never gave. Rendered, and counted as unanswered. */\\r\\nexport const UNANSWERED = \'—\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Per-question stats for a sat section, in the order the questions were served.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Pure, and shared by the results screen and the practice-test closeout so the\\r\\n * two can never compute a different row for the same section.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function buildSectionStats(\\r\\n  session: DrillSession,\\r\\n  brResults?: BlindReviewResult[],\\r\\n): SectionQuestionStat[] {\\r\\n  // Who was already answered at the buzzer. A section that never ran out has\\r\\n  // no mark, and no question on it is in overtime.\\r\\n  const atBuzzer = session.regulationMark\\r\\n    ? new Set(session.regulationMark.answeredQids)\\r\\n    : null;\\r\\n  const visited = new Set(session.visitedQids ?? []);\\r\\n\\r\\n  return session.questionQueue\\r\\n    .map((qid, idx) => {\\r\\n      const question = questionBank.getQuestion(qid);\\r\\n      const attempt = session.attempts.get(qid);\\r\\n      const brResult = brResults?.find((br) => br.qid === qid);\\r\\n\\r\\n      if (!question) return null;\\r\\n\\r\\n      // No attempt means the question was never answered, which counts as wrong.\\r\\n      return {\\r\\n        qid,\\r\\n        qnum: idx + 1,\\r\\n        qtype: question.qtype,\\r\\n        difficulty: question.difficulty,\\r\\n        initialAnswer: attempt?.selectedAnswer || UNANSWERED,\\r\\n        correctAnswer: question.correctAnswer,\\r\\n        initialCorrect: attempt?.correct || false,\\r\\n        brAnswer: brResult?.brAnswer,\\r\\n        brCorrect: brResult?.correct,\\r\\n        brChanged: brResult?.brChanged,\\r\\n        timeMs: attempt?.timeMs || 0,\\r\\n        brTimeMs: brResult?.brTimeMs,\\r\\n        switchCount: attempt?.switchCount || 0,\\r\\n        reasoningTypes: question.reasoningType?.length ? question.reasoningType : undefined,\\r\\n        questionFeatures: question.questionFeatures?.length\\r\\n          ? question.questionFeatures\\r\\n          : undefined,\\r\\n        answerTrail: attempt?.answerTrail,\\r\\n        visitCount: attempt?.visitCount,\\r\\n        noChangeRevisits: attempt?.noChangeRevisits,\\r\\n        eliminations: attempt?.eliminations,\\r\\n        // Undefined rather than false when nothing was recorded at all: a\\r\\n        // sitting from before visits were tracked must not report every\\r\\n        // question as one the student never opened.\\r\\n        everVisited: visited.size > 0 ? visited.has(qid) : undefined,\\r\\n        answeredInOvertime: atBuzzer ? !atBuzzer.has(qid) : undefined,\\r\\n        flagged: attempt?.brMarked || false,\\r\\n      };\\r\\n    })\\r\\n    .filter(Boolean) as SectionQuestionStat[];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** The scores and timings a `section_history` row is built from. */\\r\\nexport interface SectionTotals {\\r\\n  initialScore: number;\\r\\n  initialTotal: number;\\r\\n  initialPercent: number;\\r\\n  brScore: number | null;"}}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"i":2,"result":{"chunk_id":"de37b2","wall_time_seconds":6.596888,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":708,"output":"/**\\r\\n * Read one finished LR leg out of `section_history`.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Returns null when no matching row exists yet, which is the normal answer for\\r\\n * a leg the student walked away from rather than completed. The caller decides\\r\\n * what to do about that; this never invents a zero, because a zero here reads\\r\\n * as \\"answered everything wrong\\" on the score report.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function readLrLegResult(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n  pt: number,\\r\\n  section: number,\\r\\n  startedAtIso: string,\\r\\n): Promise<LegResult | null> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\'section_history\')\\r\\n    .select(\'initial_score, initial_total, total_questions, total_time_ms, time_taken_ms, completed_at\')\\r\\n    .eq(\'user_id\', userId)\\r\\n    .eq(\'pt\', pt)\\r\\n    .eq(\'section\', section)\\r\\n    .gte(\'completed_at\', startedAtIso)\\r\\n    .order(\'completed_at\', { ascending: false })\\r\\n    .limit(1);\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (error || !data || data.length === 0) return null;\\r\\n  const row = data[0];\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    correct: row.initial_score ?? 0,\\r\\n    answered: row.initial_total ?? row.total_questions ?? 0,\\r\\n    timeMs: row.total_time_ms ?? row.time_taken_ms ?? 0,\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Read one finished RC leg out of `rc_drill_sessions` + `rc_attempts`.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Matched on the passage set rather than a section number: RC sessions record\\r\\n * which passages were sat, not which section they came from.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function readRcLegResult(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n  passageIds: number[],\\r\\n  startedAtIso: string,\\r\\n): Promise<LegResult | null> {\\r\\n  const { data: sessions, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\'rc_drill_sessions\')\\r\\n    .select(\'id, passage_ids, completed_at\')\\r\\n    .eq(\'user_id\', userId)\\r\\n    .eq(\'status\', \'completed\')\\r\\n    .gte(\'completed_at\', startedAtIso)\\r\\n    .order(\'completed_at\', { ascending: false })\\r\\n    .limit(5);\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (error || !sessions || sessions.length === 0) return null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const wanted = [...passageIds].sort((a, b) => a - b).join(\',\');\\r\\n  const session = sessions.find(\\r\\n    (s) => [...(s.passage_ids ?? [])].sort((a, b) => a - b).join(\',\') === wanted,\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  if (!session) return null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data: attempts } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\'rc_attempts\')\\r\\n    .select(\'is_correct, time_ms\')\\r\\n    .eq(\'session_id\', session.id)\\r\\n    .eq(\'is_first_attempt\', true);\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (!attempts) return null;\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    correct: attempts.filter((a) => a.is_correct).length,\\r\\n    answered: attempts.length,\\r\\n    timeMs: attempts.reduce((n, a) => n + (a.time_ms ?? 0), 0),\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function readLegResult(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n  run: PracticeTestRun,\\r\\n  leg: PracticeTestLeg,\\r\\n): Promise<LegResult | null> {\\r\\n  if (!leg.startedAt) return null;\\r\\n  return leg.kind === \'lr\'\\r\\n    ? readLrLegResult(userId, run.pt, leg.section, leg.startedAt)\\r\\n    : readRcLegResult(userId, leg.passageIds ?? [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 3.9 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '    questions: stats,\r\n    // The student\'s own clock over the section\'s questions, so an accommodated\r\n    // section is judged against the time it was actually given.\r\n    paceMsPerQuestion:\r\n      minutes && stats.length ? (minutes * 60_000) / stats.length : null,\r\n    blindReviewDone: hasBlindReview(brResults),\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface PersistSectionResultsArgs {\r\n  session: DrillSession;\r\n  stats: SectionQuestionStat[];\r\n  brResults?: BlindReviewResult[];\r\n  classId: string;\r\n  /**\r\n   * Required. `section_history.user_id` has no default and no trigger, and the\r\n   * practice-test scorer matches on it.\r\n   */\r\n  userId: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Writes the `section_history` row for a sat section.\r\n *\r\n * Throws on failure. Callers decide what that means: the results screen toasts,\r\n * the practice-test closeout treats the leg as unfinished and retries.\r\n *\r\n * Returns the new row\'s id, which is what the report email is addressed by. A\r\n * caller that does not want the email can ignore it.\r\n */\r\nexport async function persistSectionResults({\r\n  session,\r\n  stats,\r\n  brResults,\r\n  classId,\r\n  userId,\r\n}: PersistSectionResultsArgs): Promise<string | null> {\r\n  const totals = buildSectionTotals(stats, brResults);\r\n\r\n  // The generated Supabase types are regenerated from the deployed schema, so\r\n  // they lag a migration that has not been applied yet. `analysis_json` is real\r\n  // in the migration and absent from the types until then; casting the row is\r\n  // the honest way to say that, rather than hand-editing a generated file.\r\n  const row = {\r\n    class_id: classId,\r\n    user_id: userId,\r\n    pt: session.fullSectionConfig?.pt || 0,\r\n    section: session.fullSectionConfig?.section || 0,\r\n    mode: session.mode,\r\n    initial_score: totals.initialScore,\r\n    initial_total: totals.initialTotal,\r\n    initial_percent: totals.initialPercent,\r\n    br_score: totals.brScore,\r\n    br_total: totals.brScore !== null ? totals.initialTotal : null,\r\n    br_percent: totals.brPercent,\r\n    br_delta: totals.delta,\r\n    total_time_ms: totals.totalTimeMs,\r\n    avg_time_ms: totals.avgTimeMs,\r\n    unanswered_count: totals.unansweredCount,\r\n    by_qtype_json: tally(stats, (s) => s.qtype),\r\n    by_difficulty_json: tally(stats, (s) => s.difficulty),\r\n    br_used: hasBlindReview(brResults),\r\n    // The per-question record. Without it the row holds totals and nothing\r\n    // else, so the Section Results table — which answer, which correct, how\r\n    // long, flagged or not — exists only for the single render that follows\r\n    // the sitting and is unrecoverable afterwards. Writing it is what makes a\r\n    // sat section a thing a student can come back to.\r\n    // Cast because `Json` demands an index signature and a named interface has\r\n    // none. The value is plain JSON-safe data; the shape is `SectionQuestionStat[]`\r\n    // and `sectionHistory.ts` reads it back as exactly that.\r\n    questions_json: stats as unknown as Json,\r\n    // The diagnosis, written once. Everything above is what happened; this is\r\n    // what it means, and it is stored rather than recomputed on read so a\r\n    // student can be shown next month exactly what they were told today.\r\n    analysis_json: analyseSection(\r\n      buildAnalysisInput(session, stats, brResults),\r\n    ) as unknown as Json,\r\n    // Legacy NOT NULL columns. Nothing writes them, so every row has carried\r\n    // two zeroes that read as "no questions, no time". `readLrLegResult` only\r\n    // survives it by falling back to initial_total / total_time_ms; any query\r\n    // that trusts these instead gets nothing. Same numbers, stated honestly.\r\n    total_questions: totals.initialTotal,\r\n    time_taken_ms: totals.totalTimeMs,\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n    .from(\'section_history\')\r\n    .insert(row as unknown as never)\r\n    .select(\'id\')\r\n    .maybeSingle();\r\n\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  return (data as { id?: string } | null)?.id ?? null;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Sends the teacher the finished section: the whole record, then the diagnosis.\r\n *\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 9.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "/**\r\n * Persistence factory.\r\n *\r\n * For signed-in users we use SupabaseSyncedPersistence: local-first reads/writes\r\n * with background autosave to the `bootcamp_kv` table so every profile keeps\r\n * all of their bootcamp progress in the cloud and across devices.\r\n * For anonymous callers we fall back to local-only V1Persistence.\r\n */\r\n\r\nimport type { Persistence } from './Persistence';\r\nimport { V1Persistence } from './V1Persistence';\r\nimport { LocalStoragePersistence } from './LocalStoragePersistence';\r\nimport { IndexedDBPersistence } from './IndexedDBPersistence';\r\nimport { SupabaseSyncedPersistence } from './SupabaseSyncedPersistence';\r\n\r\nconst _cache: Map<string, Persistence> = new Map();\r\nlet _testOverride: Persistence | null = null;\r\n\r\n/** Returns the active adapter scoped to the supplied userId. */\r\nexport function getPersistence(userId: string = 'anon'): Persistence {\r\n  if (_testOverride) return _testOverride;\r\n  const existing = _cache.get(userId);\r\n  if (existing) return existing;\r\n  const created: Persistence =\r\n    userId && userId !== 'anon'\r\n      ? new SupabaseSyncedPersistence(userId)\r\n      : new V1Persistence(\r\n          new LocalStoragePersistence(userId),\r\n          new IndexedDBPersistence(userId),\r\n        );\r\n  _cache.set(userId, created);\r\n  return created;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Test hook — inject a fake. Passing null clears any override and cache. */\r\nexport function __setPersistenceForTests(p: Persistence | null): void {\r\n  _testOverride = p;\r\n  if (p === null) _cache.clear();\r\n}\r\n\r\n"}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 4.1 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "/**\r\n * Was a blind review actually done? One predicate, used by both the totals and\r\n * the persisted row, so the score on screen and the row in section_history can\r\n * never disagree about whether a review happened.\r\n */\r\nconst hasBlindReview = (brResults?: BlindReviewResult[]): boolean =>\r\n  Array.isArray(brResults) && brResults.length > 0;\r\n\r\nexport function buildSectionTotals(\r\n  stats: SectionQuestionStat[],\r\n  brResults?: BlindReviewResult[],\r\n): SectionTotals {\r\n  const initialTotal = stats.length;\r\n  const initialScore = stats.filter((s) => s.initialCorrect).length;\r\n  const initialPercent = initialTotal\r\n    ? Math.round((initialScore / initialTotal) * 100)\r\n    : 0;\r\n\r\n  // An EMPTY array is not a blind review. `brResults ?` treated `[]` as one,\r\n  // so a student who skipped straight to the score report was told they had\r\n  // scored 0 on a review they never sat, with a delta to match. Null is the\r\n  // only honest answer when there is nothing to report.\r\n  const brScore = hasBlindReview(brResults) ? stats.filter((s) => s.brCorrect).length : null;\r\n  const brPercent =\r\n    brScore !== null && initialTotal\r\n      ? Math.round((brScore / initialTotal) * 100)\r\n      : null;\r\n  const delta = brScore !== null ? brScore - initialScore : null;\r\n\r\n  const totalTimeMs = stats.reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.timeMs, 0);\r\n  const avgTimeMs = initialTotal ? Math.round(totalTimeMs / initialTotal) : 0;\r\n  const unansweredCount = stats.filter(\r\n    (s) => s.initialAnswer === UNANSWERED,\r\n  ).length;\r\n\r\n  return {\r\n    initialScore,\r\n    initialTotal,\r\n    initialPercent,\r\n    brScore,\r\n    brPercent,\r\n    delta,\r\n    totalTimeMs,\r\n    avgTimeMs,\r\n    unansweredCount,\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** A `{ correct, total }` tally keyed however the caller groups the stats. */\r\nfunction tally<K extends string | number>(\r\n  stats: SectionQuestionStat[],\r\n  key: (s: SectionQuestionStat) => K,\r\n): Record<K, { correct: number; total: number }> {\r\n  const out = {} as Record<K, { correct: number; total: number }>;\r\n  stats.forEach((s) => {\r\n    const k = key(s);\r\n    if (!out[k]) out[k] = { correct: 0, total: 0 };\r\n    out[k].total++;\r\n    if (s.initialCorrect) out[k].correct++;\r\n  });\r\n  return out;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The analysis input for a sat section, enriched from the question bank.\r\n *\r\n * The bank already knows which reasoning each question runs on — conditional,\r\n * causal, quantifier — and that is the cleanest signal we have for a\r\n * fundamentals gap, which is otherwise the hardest category to tell apart from\r\n * a process one. It has been sitting unread; this is what reads it.\r\n */\r\nexport function buildAnalysisInput(\r\n  session: DrillSession,\r\n  stats: SectionQuestionStat[],\r\n  brResults?: BlindReviewResult[],\r\n): AnalysisInput {\r\n  const timer = session.fullSectionConfig?.timer;\r\n  const minutes =\r\n    !timer || timer.mode === 'unlimited'\r\n      ? null\r\n      : timer.mode === 'custom'\r\n        ? (timer.customMinutes ?? null)\r\n        : Number(timer.mode);\r\n\r\n  return {\r\n"}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 2.1 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '/**\r\n * Persistence interface — the only contract app code talks to.\r\n * v1 ships V1Persistence (LocalStorage + IndexedDB). v1.5 / LSAT U absorption\r\n * swaps in SupabasePersistence as a one-file change in `factory.ts`.\r\n *\r\n * See: docs/architecture-plan.md §3 + spec.html §6.9.\r\n */\r\n\r\nexport interface Persistence {\r\n  /**\r\n   * Resolves once the backing store is safe to read for the first time.\r\n   * V1Persistence (local-only) is always immediately ready. SupabaseSyncedPersistence\r\n   * resolves this after its initial cloud hydrate() completes (or definitively fails),\r\n   * so callers can await it before deciding a record is missing. Prevents fabricating\r\n   * a default row over real cloud progress on a cold cross-device load.\r\n   */\r\n  ready(): Promise<void>;\r\n\r\n  // Tabular records (KV by table + record id)\r\n  get<T>(table: string, id: string): Promise<T | null>;\r\n  set<T>(table: string, id: string, record: T): Promise<void>;\r\n  remove(table: string, id: string): Promise<void>;\r\n  list<T>(table: string, filter?: Partial<T>): Promise<T[]>;\r\n\r\n  // Blob storage (R&R audio, large binaries)\r\n  putBlob(key: string, blob: Blob): Promise<void>;\r\n  getBlob(key: string): Promise<Blob | null>;\r\n  removeBlob(key: string): Promise<void>;\r\n\r\n  // Batched writes\r\n  transaction<T>(fn: (tx: PersistenceTx) => Promise<T>): Promise<T>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface PersistenceTx {\r\n  get<T>(table: string, id: string): Promise<T | null>;\r\n  set<T>(table: string, id: string, record: T): Promise<void>;\r\n}\r\n'}]

## assistant

Implemented account-backed LR and RC section resume, saved clocks, historical section listings, and full-exam device handoff guards. Typecheck and 14 focused tests pass. Existing full-exam LR resume rows lack run identity, so those older rows cannot be safely assigned to a specific exam automatically.

## assistant

I found both paths. I’m carrying the exam identity through RC navigation and changing LR hydration so a delayed cloud read cannot replace newer answers or clock time saved on this device.

Assigning one\\r\\n * activity must open that activity, not the Activity Centre; assigning one\\r\\n * PrepTest must open that sitting, not the Testing Centre. So an assignment\\r\\n * authorises its own runner: this gate lets the child render when the feature\\r\\n * switch is on OR when `authorize` proves the signed-in student owns the\\r\\n * specific thing in the route. Otherwise it falls through to the ordinary\\r\\n * ProtectedRoute, which shows the locked state.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Same shape as BootcampGate, which has done this for bootcamps all along.\\r\\n * Route-level only: RLS already scopes every assignment table by student_id,\\r\\n * so this decides what a student is shown, not what they can read.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function AssignmentGate({\\r\\n  flag,\\r\\n  authKey,\\r\\n  authorize,\\r\\n  children,\\r\\n}: {\\r\\n  flag: PermissionFlag | readonly PermissionFlag[];\\r\\n  /** Stable name for the thing being authorised, for caching. null = nothing to authorise. */\\r\\n  authKey: string | null;\\r\\n  authorize: (userId: string) => Promise<boolean>;\\r\\n  children: ReactNode;\\r\\n}) {\\r\\n  const { user, authReady } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\\r\\n  const flags = (Array.isArray(flag) ? flag : [flag]) as PermissionFlag[];\\r\\n  const flagOpen = permissions.is_admin || flags.some((f) => Boolean(permissions[f]));\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data: assigned, isLoading } = useQuery<boolean>({\\r\\n    queryKey: [\\"assignment-gate\\", user?.id, authKey],\\r\\n    enabled: authReady && !!user && !permissions.loading && !flagOpen && !!authKey,\\r\\n    queryFn: () => authorize(user!.id),\\r\\n    staleTime: 60_000,\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  const waiting =\\r\\n    authReady && !!user && !permissions.loading && !flagOpen && !!authKey && isLoading;\\r\\n  if (waiting) {\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background flex items-center justify-center\\">\\r\\n        <div className=\\"w-6 h-6 border-2 border-muted border-t-foreground rounded-full animate-spin\\" />\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (!flagOpen && assigned) return <>{children}</>;\\r\\n  return <ProtectedRoute flag={flag}>{children}</ProtectedRoute>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// ── What each runner accepts as proof ──────────────────────────────────────\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function ownsRow(table: string, id: string, userId: string): Promise<boolean> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(table as never)\\r\\n    .select(\\"id\\")\\r\\n    .eq(\\"id\\", id)\\r\\n    .eq(\\"student_id\\", userId)\\r\\n    .maybeSingle();\\r\\n  if (error) return false;\\r\\n  return !!data;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** A live practice-test assignment for exactly this PrepTest. */\\r\\nexport async function hasPracticeTestAssignment(userId: string, pt?: number): Promise<boolean> {\\r\\n  let query = supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"practice_test_assignments\\" as never)\\r\\n    .select(\\"id\\")\\r\\n    .eq(\\"student_id\\", userId)\\r\\n    .neq(\\"status\\", \\"archived\\");\\r\\n  if (typeof pt === \\"number\\") query = query.eq(\\"pt\\", pt);\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await query.limit(1);\\r\\n  if (error) return false;\\r\\n  return (data ?? []).length > 0;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function hasAssignedBootcamp(userId: string): Promise<boolean> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"classroom_items\\")\\r\\n    .select(\\"id\\")\\r\\n    .eq(\\"student_id\\", userId)\\r\\n    .eq(\\"type\\", \\"bootcamp_module\\")\\r\\n    .neq(\\"status\\", \\"archived\\")\\r\\n    .limit(1);\\r\\n  if (error) return false;\\r\\n  return (data ?? []).length > 0;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** The PrepTest a sitting leg belongs to, read from the run on this device. */\\r\\nfunction ptOfRun(runId: string | null | undefined): number | null {\\r\\n  if (!runId) return null;\\r\\n  return loadPracticeTestRun(runId)?.pt ?? null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// ── Route wrappers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\r\\n\\r\\n/** /activities/:assignmentId */\\r\\nexport function ActivityAssignmentGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\\r\\n  const { assignmentId } = useParams<{ assignmentId: string }>();\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <AssignmentGate\\r\\n      flag=\\"has_activity_access\\"\\r\\n      authKey={assignmentId ? `activity:${assignmentId}` : null}\\r\\n      authorize={(uid) => ownsRow(\\"activity_assignments\\", assignmentId!, uid)}\\r\\n    >\\r\\n      {children}\\r\\n    </AssignmentGate>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** /classroom/ta/:assignmentId */\\r\\nexport function TAAssignmentGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\\r\\n  const { assignmentId } = useParams<{ assignmentId: string }>();\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <AssignmentGate\\r\\n      flag=\\"has_ta_access\\"\\r\\n      authKey={assignmentId ? `ta:${assignmentId}` : null}\\r\\n      authorize={(uid) => ownsRow(\\"ta_assignments\\", assignmentId!, uid)}\\r\\n    >\\r\\n      {children}\\r\\n    </AssignmentGate>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** /testing: the Testing Centre lists only assigned PrepTests without the switch. */\\r\\nexport function TestingCentreGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <AssignmentGate\\r\\n      flag=\\"has_practice_access\\"\\r\\n      authKey=\\"testing:any\\"\\r\\n      authorize={(uid) => hasPracticeTestAssignment(uid)}\\r\\n    >\\r\\n      {children}\\r\\n    </AssignmentGate>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** /testing/test/:runId */\\r\\nexport function PracticeTestRunGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\\r\\n  const { runId } = useParams<{ runId: string }>();\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <AssignmentGate\\r\\n      flag=\\"has_practice_access\\"\\r\\n      authKey={runId ? `practice-run:${runId}` : null}\\r\\n      authorize={async (uid) => {\\r\\n        if (!runId) return false;\\r\\n        const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n          .from(\\"practice_test_runs\\" as never)\\r\\n          .select(\\"pt,status\\")\\r\\n          .eq(\\"id\\", runId)\\r\\n          .eq(\\"user_id\\", uid)\\r\\n          .maybeSingle();\\r\\n        if (!error && data) {\\r\\n          const accountRun = data as { pt: number; status: string };\\r\\n          return accountRun.status === \\"complete\\" ||\\r\\n            hasPracticeTestAssignment(uid, accountRun.pt);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        const localPt = ptOfRun(runId);\\r\\n        return localPt !== null && hasPracticeTestAssignment(uid, localPt);\\r\\n      }}\\r\\n    >\\r\\n      {children}\\r\\n    </AssignmentGate>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * What an assignment-launched drill run is authorised by, if anything. The UI does not block on it, but callers that\\r\\n    // must not fabricate a default over real cloud data await ready() first.\\r\\n    // hydrate() never rejects (it catches internally), so ready() always resolves.\\r\\n    this.readyPromise = this.hydrate();\\r\\n\\r\\n    // Best-effort flush on tab close.\\r\\n    if (typeof window !== \'undefined\') {\\r\\n      window.addEventListener(\'beforeunload\', () => {\\r\\n        void this.flush();\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      window.addEventListener(\'visibilitychange\', () => {\\r\\n        if (document.visibilityState === \'hidden\') void this.flush();\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  ready(): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    return this.readyPromise;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  private key(table: string, id: string): string {\\r\\n    return `${table}::${id}`;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async hydrate(): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    if (this.hydrated || !this.userId || this.userId === \'anon\') return;\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\'bootcamp_kv\')\\r\\n        .select(\'table_name, record_id, data\')\\r\\n        .eq(\'user_id\', this.userId);\\r\\n      if (error) {\\r\\n        console.warn(\'[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] hydrate failed\', error.message);\\r\\n        return;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      for (const row of data ?? []) {\\r\\n        try {\\r\\n          await this.local.set(row.table_name, row.record_id, row.data as object);\\r\\n        } catch (e) {\\r\\n          console.warn(\'[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] hydrate row failed\', e);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      this.hydrated = true;\\r\\n    } catch (e) {\\r\\n      console.warn(\'[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] hydrate threw\', e);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  private scheduleFlush(): void {\\r\\n    if (this.flushTimer) clearTimeout(this.flushTimer);\\r\\n    this.flushTimer = setTimeout(() => {\\r\\n      void this.flush();\\r\\n    }, FLUSH_DEBOUNCE_MS);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async flush(): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    if (!this.userId || this.userId === \'anon\') return;\\r\\n    if (this.pending.size === 0) return;\\r\\n    const batch = Array.from(this.pending.values());\\r\\n    this.pending.clear();\\r\\n    if (this.flushTimer) {\\r\\n      clearTimeout(this.flushTimer);\\r\\n      this.flushTimer = null;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    const upserts = batch.filter((b) => b.data !== null);\\r\\n    const deletes = batch.filter((b) => b.data === null);\\r\\n\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      if (upserts.length > 0) {\\r\\n        const rows = upserts.map((b) => ({\\r\\n          user_id: this.userId,\\r\\n          table_name: b.table,\\r\\n          record_id: b.id,\\r\\n          data: b.data as never,\\r\\n        }));\\r\\n        const { error } = await supabase\\r\\n          .from(\'bootcamp_kv\')\\r\\n          .upsert(rows, { onConflict: \'user_id,table_name,record_id\' });\\r\\n        if (error) {\\r\\n          console.warn(\'[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] upsert failed\', error.message);\\r\\n          // Re-queue on failure.\\r\\n          for (const b of upserts) this.pending.set(this.key(b.table, b.id), b);\\r\\n          this.scheduleFlush();\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      for (const d of deletes) {\\r\\n        const { error } = await supabase\\r\\n          .from(\'bootcamp_kv\')\\r\\n          .delete()\\r\\n          .eq(\'user_id\', this.userId)\\r\\n          .eq(\'table_name\', d.table)\\r\\n          .eq(\'record_id\', d.id);\\r\\n        if (error) {\\r\\n          // Student deletes are denied by RLS (admin-only data retention).\\r\\n          // Drop the delete rather than requeueing forever — the cloud row is\\r\\n          // intentionally preserved.\\r\\n          console.warn(\'[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] delete denied/failed (cloud row preserved)\', error.message);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    } catch (e) {\\r\\n      console.warn(\'[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] flush threw\', e);\\r\\n      for (const b of batch) this.pending.set(this.key(b.table, b.id), b);\\r\\n      this.scheduleFlush();\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // ── Persistence API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\r\\n\\r\\n  get<T>(table: string, id: string): Promise<T | null> {\\r\\n    return this.local.get<T>(table, id);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async set<T>(table: string, id: string, record: T): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    await this.local.set(table, id, record);\\r\\n    this.pending.set(this.key(table, id), { table, id, data: record });\\r\\n    this.scheduleFlush();\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async remove(table: string, id: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    await
